A directory entry covers everything\n161\t * beneath it.\n162\t *\n163\t * Staging walks these beside {@link HOST_PATHS}, so a release ships them and a\n164\t * reader reaches them two ways: a scaffold checkout sitting beside the\n165\t * repository, or the `node_modules/@orkestrel/scaffold/dist/host/` root inside\n166\t * the installed package. A workspace therefore stops receiving later\n212\t * canonical updates to them. `.gitignore` takes that trade because its correct\n213\t * rules differ by workspace, so scaffold cannot own its bytes.\n214\t */\n215\texport const WORKSPACE_OWNED_PATHS: readonly string[] = Object.freeze(['.gitignore'])\n216\t\n217\t/**\n218\t * The vendored paths a target receives with its executable bit set, frozen.\n219\t *\n220\t * @remarks\n221\t * Declared rather than read from the staging host's filesystem, because that\n222\t * reading is not portable: Windows carries no executable bit, so a host staged\n223\t * there reports every file non-executable and every target receives hooks it\n224\t * cannot run. Declaring the set here makes one checkout stage one manifest on\n225\t * every host.\n226\t *\n227\t * Every entry is also a {@link HOST_PATHS} member or sits beneath one. A file\n228\t * that must run when a target invokes it belongs here the moment it is vendored.\n229\t */\n230\texport const EXECUTABLE_PATHS: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([\n231\t\t'scripts/codex.sh',\n232\t\t'scripts/cursor.sh',\n233\t\t'scripts/deps.sh',\n234\t\t'scripts/ollama.sh',\n235\t])\n236\t\n237\t/**\n238\t * The path prefixes whose contents instruct or wire an agent, frozen.\n239\t *\n240\t * @remarks\n241\t * A path is grouped by what it governs rather than by where it sits: anything\n242\t * beneath one of these prefixes is `orchestration`, and everything else that is\n243\t * not source, tests, guides, docs, or a manifest is `configs`. A vendored path\n244\t * and a foreign path found in a target are classified against the same list, so\n245\t * a new harness directory is admitted once.\n246\t */\n247\texport const ORCHESTRATION_PATH_PREFIXES: readonly string[] = Object.freeze([\n248\t\t'.agents/',\n249\t\t'.claude/',\n250\t\t'.codex/',\n251\t\t'.cursor/',\n252\t\t'.github/',\n253\t\t'scripts/',\n254\t])\n255\t\n256\t/**\n257\t * The exact root filenames that wire an agent bench rather than the toolchain, frozen.\n258\t *\n259\t * @remarks\n260\t * `.mcp.json` registers MCP servers for the harness. It sits among the root\n261\t * dotfiles but governs agents, so it groups with the harness bridges.\n262\t */\n263\texport const ORCHESTRATION_PATH_NAMES: readonly string[] = Object.freeze(['.mcp.json'])\n264\t\n265\t/**\n266\t * The agent file whose marker-bounded package table the catalog verb alone owns.\n267\t *\n268\t * @remarks\n269\t * A plan claims it at a canon path, because the catalog verb refuses a target\n270\t * that lacks the file. `nameToHostArtifacts` appends it to the vendored\n271\t * selection, and it reaches a release through the `.claude/agents` directory in\n272\t * {@link CANON_PATHS} rather than through {@link HOST_PATHS}, which is what\n273\t * keeps the two lists disjoint.\n274\t *\n275\t * It is claimed by presence rather than content, so a consumer's own edits to\n276\t * the file survive every verb and only the catalog verb rewrites the region\n277\t * inside the markers.\n278\t */\n279\texport const CATALOG_AGENT_PATH = '.claude/agents/orkestrel.md'\n280\t\n281\t/** The provisioner skeleton a workspace with declared service vendors is given once. */\n282\texport const SERVICE_SCRIPT_PATH = 'scripts/service.sh'\n283\t\n284\t/** The shared Vitest global-setup module whose presence makes a workspace `global`. */\n285\texport const GLOBAL_SETUP_PATH = 'tests/setupGlobal.ts'\n286\t\n287\t/** The guide-parity proof whose presence selects the planned `guides` project. */\n288\texport const GUIDES_TEST_PATH = 'tests/guides.test.ts'\n289\t\n290\t/** The generated packed-package proof every publishing workspace is planned at. */\n291\texport const DISTRIBUTION_TEST_PATH = 'tests/distribution.test.ts'\n292\t\n293\t/**\n294\t * The `prepublishOnly` row that runs the packed-package proof against a real registry.\n295\t *\n296\t * @remarks\n297\t * The proof reads `import.meta.env.MODE`, so without `--mode release` it passes\n298\t * on an unreachable registry instead of failing. The row therefore has one home\n299\t * and both the script compiler and the manifest region writer read it from here.\n300\t */\n301\texport const RELEASE_PROOF_COMMAND = 'npm run test:distribution -- --mode release'\n302\t\n303\t/** The cross-environment composition proof whose presence makes a workspace `integration`. */\n304\texport const INTEGRATION_TEST_PATH = 'tests/integration.test.ts'\n305\t\n306\t/** The manifest path every compiler plan emits with birth ownership. */\n307\texport const MANIFEST_PATH = 'package.json'\n308\t\n309\t/** The official-tooling drift proof whose presence makes a workspace `conformance`. */"}]},"uuid":"d34f5fe7-4e1a-4541-b1e8-d473da72dd77","timestamp":"2026-08-27T09:28:36.080Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9820944d-8234-4d7c-b757-d719c0e729c0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote","cwd":"/home/user/scaffold","sessionId":"dc70cba3-3e2a-551f-9e05-1da04bbbf122","version":"2.1.247","gitBranch":"claude/scaffold-proposal-impl-nabmm9"}
